The latter is made accessible at termination by introducing an auxiliary state variable that records the players' minimum separation distance throughout their flight. This novel combination of termination condition and auxiliary state removes a structural limitation of current pursuit games and opens a new class of solvable problems. It also enables us to embed attacker mission constraints directly. Taking an indirect approach, we formulate the game's Two-Point Boundary Value Problem, solve it numerically, and identify several solution types. When the Attacker's only constraint is to reach the Target, he can do so without being captured. These types of solutions, however, are less practical due to long flight times and slow terminal speeds. Imposing an Attacker terminal speed constraint yields simpler solutions and reveals the trade-off between terminal speed and flyby distance. We demonstrate that in such a setting, capture is possible in a relevant region of the parameter space.

</details>


### [221] [A Survey of Community Detection from an Operations Research Perspective: Taxonomy, Mathematical Formulations, Modularity Functions, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13031)
*Miguel A. Pozo Montaño,Sebastián V Taboh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对网络科学中的社区检测问题进行了系统性综述，重点聚焦于基于模块度的方法。研究提出了一个统一的概念框架，涵盖了多维分类法、通用数学形式化、模块度函数分析、计算方法综述以及基准数据集评估，为研究人员和实践者提供了结构化的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社区检测文献缺乏统一的分类框架和概念体系，不同研究在分类标准上存在显著差异，阻碍了领域知识的系统整合与比较。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，首先分析现有调查文献及其分类标准，进而提出一个多维分类法；随后建立兼容多种社区结构（不交叠、重叠、模糊）的通用数学形式化模型；在此基础上系统评述代表性模块度函数及其假设与局限，并分别从算法和数学规划两个角度梳理基于模块度的社区检测方法；最后回顾常用基准数据集及其在评估和可重复性中的作用。

Result: 提出了一个包含网络特征、社区结构、目标函数、方法论范式、评估标准和应用领域六个维度的分类框架；建立了统一的社区检测问题数学形式化；系统分析了多种模块度函数及其空模型假设和已知局限；区分了算法方法和数学规划方法两大技术路线。

Conclusion: 本文通过整合分类学、数学建模、模块度分析和基准测试实践，为社区检测及网络优化相关问题提供了一个结构化的综合参考框架，有助于推动该领域理论研究和实际应用的进一步发展。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental problem in network science that consists of identifying groups of vertices exhibiting stronger internal connectivity than external connectivity. From an Operations Research perspective, the problem can be interpreted as a family of combinatorial optimization and clustering models defined over network structures. This survey provides a unified framework for the study of community detection, with particular emphasis on modularity-based approaches. We first review existing surveys and analyze the taxonomic criteria used to classify the literature, highlighting the absence of a common conceptual framework. Based on this analysis, we propose a multidimensional taxonomy that organizes community detection methods according to network characteristics, community structure, objective functions, methodological paradigms, evaluation criteria, and application domains. We then introduce a general mathematical formalization of the Community Detection Problem that accommodates disjoint, overlapping, and fuzzy community structures within a unified assignment framework. Building on this formalization, we review representative modularity functions, discussing their underlying assumptions, null models, and known limitations. We also survey modularity-based community detection methods, distinguishing between algorithmic and mathematical programming approaches. Finally, we review commonly used benchmark datasets and discuss their role in evaluation and reproducibility. By integrating taxonomy, mathematical modeling, modularity analysis, and benchmarking practices, this survey provides a structured reference for researchers and practitioners working on community detection and related network optimization problems.

</details>


### [222] [Scheduling of Star Observations under Uncertain Conditions: A Comparison of Models and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13058)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究望远镜观测恒星调度问题，将夜晚视为机器、恒星视为作业，在未知实际可用夜晚数量（受气象条件影响）的鲁棒优化框架下，最大化观测总利润，并评估最坏情况下的调度质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际望远镜观测调度中，气象和大气条件不可预知，导致实际可用观测夜晚数量不确定。传统调度方法无法处理这种不确定性，需要一种在未知机器数量下仍能保证高质量调度的鲁棒方案。

Method: 将问题建模为并行机调度：夜晚=机器，恒星=作业（有可见时间窗和最小观测时长约束）。假设每夜要么完美（全部可观测），要么糟糕（无可观测）。采用鲁棒优化范式，评估最坏情况下的调度性能。

Result: 在鲁棒优化框架下，针对未知可用夜晚数量的调度问题，给出了最坏情况下的性能评估结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为不确定环境下望远镜观测调度提供了鲁棒优化方法，能够在实际可用夜晚数量未知时仍保证最坏情况下的调度质量，对天文观测资源规划具有实际意义。

Abstract: We consider a scheduling problem for star observations using a telescope. Identical nights are available to observe stars that have been identified as of interest by astronomers. Each star yields a profit if it is observed; in that case, the observation must respect a visibility window and a minimum observation duration. The objective is to maximize the total profit of the observations that are actually performed. By interpreting nights as machines and stars as jobs to be processed, this problem is a classic scheduling setting of parallel machines. In the practical star-observation problem, the ability to observe depends on meteorological and atmospheric conditions, which are not known when the schedule is computed. A possible modeling is to consider that each night can be either perfect and everything is observable, either terrible and nothing is observable. The challenge is therefore to propose a ''high-quality'' schedule without knowing the actual number of nights (machines) that will ultimately be available. We focus here on evaluating the worst case within the robust optimization paradigm.

</details>


### [223] [A symmetric Gauss-Seidel based alternating proximal ALM for generalized Nash Equilibrium problems in Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13198)
*Defeng Sun,Hailing Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有联合线性约束的单调广义纳什均衡问题(GNEPs)，提出了一种新的基于对称高斯-赛德尔(sGS)的交替邻近增广拉格朗日方法(sGS-APALM)，通过设计二次代理函数实现玩家策略的交替更新，避免了耦合子问题求解，并在仅需伪梯度映射的单调性和Lipschitz连续性假设下建立了全局收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理GNEPs时通常需要求解耦合的纳什均衡子问题，计算代价高昂；且许多基于分裂的方法依赖强单调性或共强制等更强的假设，限制了其适用性。本文旨在设计一种更高效、假设更弱的算法。

Method: 提出sGS-APALM方法，核心是引入新设计的二次代理函数，结合对称高斯-赛德尔交替更新机制，将原始GNEP转化为一系列无约束二次规划子问题，实现玩家策略的交替求解。

Result: 在仅需伪梯度映射单调性和Lipschitz连续的假设下建立了全局收敛性分析并给出了收敛率估计；将方法应用于一类带联合状态约束的风险中性PDE约束GNEP，初步数值实验验证了方法的效率和有效性。

Conclusion: sGS-APALM为求解单调GNEPs提供了一种高效、可替代的数值方案，特别适用于玩家策略空间为Hilbert空间、联合约束在Banach空间中表述的复杂问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a class of monotone generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) with jointly linear constraints. The players' strategy spaces are real Hilbert spaces, while the joint constraint is formulated in a Banach space. To solve such problems, we propose a novel symmetric Gauss-Seidel (sGS) based alternating proximal augmented Lagrangian method (sGS-APALM) which incorporates newly designed quadratic surrogates. In contrast to existing regularization and ALM-type methods, the proposed method avoids solving coupled Nash equilibrium subproblems at each iteration and instead updates the players' strategies alternately by solving a sequence of unconstrained quadratic programs. Moreover, unlike many existing splitting-based methods, our global convergence analysis and convergence rate estimation require only monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the pseudo-gradient mapping, without imposing stronger assumptions such as strong monotonicity or co-coercivity. Finally, we apply the method to a class of risk-neutral PDE-constrained GNEPs with joint state constraints, and preliminary numerical results demonstrate its efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [224] [A smoothing extended sequential quadratic method for difference-of-convex optimization over a convex composite inequality constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13343)
*Jiefeng Xu,Ting Kei Pong,Yongle Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对差值凸目标函数在凸复合不等式约束和紧凸集约束下的极小化问题，提出了一种融合变分平滑策略的扩展ESQM算法，建立了$O(ε^{-3})$的迭代复杂度以获得$(ε,ε)$-KKT点，并在凸情形下证明了全序列收敛性及Hölderian增长条件下的局部收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ESQM算法无法有效处理差值凸目标函数与非光滑凸复合约束的组合问题，需要更高效的算法框架。

Method: 在ESQM框架中引入变分平滑机制，每次迭代对基于凸复合约束函数光滑逼近构造的惩罚函数执行一步近端梯度更新，并设计显式规则更新平滑参数和惩罚参数。

Result: 在适当约束规范下，获得$(ε,ε)$-KKT点的迭代复杂度为$O(ε^{-3})$；凸情形下全序列收敛，且在标准Hölderian增长条件下导出局部收敛速率。

Conclusion: 所提算法能有效求解差值凸优化问题，复杂度分析与收敛性理论完备。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing a difference-of-convex objective over a convex composite inequality constraint and a compact convex set constraint. To solve this problem, we extend the ESQM in [1] via incorporating a variable smoothing scheme. In essence, in each iteration of our algorithm, we apply one proximal gradient step to a smoothed penalty function, constructed based on a smooth approximation of the convex composite constraint function; and we design explicit rules to update the smoothing and penalty parameters. Under suitable constraint qualifications, we establish an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for obtaining an $(ε,ε)$-KKT point. Moreover, in the convex setting, we show that the whole sequence generated by our algorithm is convergent and derive its local convergence rate under a standard Hölderian growth condition.

</details>


### [225] [Coordinate-wise Polyhedral Method for Eliciting Multivariate Linear Utility and Univariate Nonlinear Utility Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13418)
*Jiaxin Wei,Jia Liu,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出坐标方式多面体方法(CPM)用于切割多面体，通过预先指定坐标切割并求解线性方程组设计查询，实现线性收敛率，并扩展到单变量分段线性效用函数和一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数，理论分析和数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多面体方法通过求解耦合优化问题设计成对比较查询，计算复杂度高。CPM旨在通过预先指定坐标切割并求解线性方程组来降低计算成本，同时保证理论收敛性。

Method: CPM方法：(1) 预先指定坐标切割，通过求解线性方程组设计对应的成对比较查询；(2) 将CPM扩展到单变量分段线性效用函数，利用增量向量表示；(3) 通过分段线性近似(PLA)扩展到一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数，并引入自适应断点策略避免方向误差。

Result: (1) CPM以线性收敛率减小多面体的直径；(2) 真实效用函数与CPM估计值之间的Kantorovich距离以线性收敛率递减；(3) 分段线性效用函数集合收敛到真实效用函数；(4) 数值实验显示CPM在线性多变量效用函数上与标准多面体方法收敛性相当，对非线性单变量效用函数实现稳定收敛。

Conclusion: CPM是一种高效的多面体切割方法，具有线性收敛保证，可扩展到多种效用函数形式，理论结果与实验验证一致。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a coordinate-wise polyhedral method (CPM) for cutting polyhedra with theoretical guarantees of convergence. Unlike the existing polyhedral method, which designs pairwise comparison queries by solving coupled optimization problems and performs cuts subsequently, CPM specifies coordinate-wise cuts in advance and then designs corresponding pairwise comparison queries by solving a linear system of equations. Under this framework, we show that CPM reduces the diameter of the polyhedron at a linear convergence rate. Moreover, we extend CPM to a univariate piecewise-linear utility function by representing it with its increment vector over consecutive breakpoints. We show that the Kantorovich distance between the true utility function and the estimated one obtained by CPM decreases at a linear convergence rate. Further, we extend the CPM to general nondecreasing Lipschitz continuous utility functions by piecewise-linear approximation (PLA). We introduce an adaptive-breakpoint strategy to avoid direction errors caused by PLA. We prove that the set of piecewise-linear utility functions corresponding to the ambiguity set of increment vectors converges to the true utility function as the number of queries increases, and derive an explicit bound for the approximation. Finally, to evaluate the performance of CPM, we conduct a series of numerical experiments. The results demonstrate comparable convergence to the standard polyhedral method in the case of linear multivariate utility functions. For nonlinear univariate utility functions, CPM achieves stable convergence to the true utility function, in line with the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [226] [Towards a Control interpretation of Quantum Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13481)
*Dario Pighin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于控制理论的量子优势（QA）分析框架，通过将量子计算转化为SU(N)群上的算子能控性问题，并用多项式时间上界刻画量子优势。分别在超导数字量子处理器上的量子傅里叶变换（QFT）和中性原子模拟量子处理器上的最大独立集（MIS）问题中验证了该框架，证明了能控性并推导了时间上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子优势的研究缺乏统一的理论框架来系统地表征量子优势何时以及如何产生，作者希望利用控制理论为量子优势提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 采用双线性受控薛定谔方程作为主线，将目标量子计算重新表述为特殊酉群SU(N)上的算子能控性问题，量子优势被定义为关于最小时间函数的n的多项式上界。通过李代数论证证明算子能控性，并利用门级联引理和标准电路分解推导时间上界。

Result: a) 对于超导数字量子处理器上的QFT，证明了算子能控性并得到O(n²)的时间上界；b) 对于中性原子模拟量子处理器上的MIS问题，将Rydberg阻塞哈密顿量分析为双线性控制系统，将QAOA重新表述为连续时间最优控制问题，并提出了基于控制的量子优势定义。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子优势提供了控制理论视角，并在两个典型问题上得到验证。最后提出了若干开放问题，为控制理论与量子计算交叉领域未来的研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We develop a control-theoretic framework for understanding Quantum Advantage (QA), providing a systematic route to characterize when and how QA can arise. The bilinear controlled Schrödinger equation is the common thread: the target quantum computation is recast as an operator controllability problem on the special unitary group $SU(N)$, and QA is identified with a polynomial-in-$n$ upper bound on the associated minimal-time function.
  We illustrate the framework on two paradigmatic problems: a) the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) on superconducting digital quantum processors (such as IBM's ibm_brisbane), for which we prove operator controllability by a Lie-algebraic argument and derive an $O(n^2)$ upper bound on the minimal time via a gate-concatenation lemma combined with the standard QFT circuit decomposition; b) the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem on neutral-atom analog quantum processors (such as Pasqal's hardware), for which we analyze the Rydberg-blockade Hamiltonian as a bilinear control system and reformulate the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) as a continuous-time optimal control problem. By a controllability result, we show how the problem can be solved on Pasqal Quantum Computers and we introduce a control-based definition of Quantum Advantage for MIS.
  We conclude by outlining several open problems that chart directions for future research at the intersection of Control Theory and Quantum Computing.

</details>


### [227] [A Sustainable Integrated Framework for Multi-Type Urban Waste Collection and Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13524)
*Víctor Blanco,J. Fernando Camacho-Vallejo,Yolanda Hinojosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成优化框架，用于设计多类型城市垃圾收集与回收系统，通过联合确定中转设施和处理厂的位置及两层级路径规划，在有限预算下最小化总运营成本。基于哥伦比亚麦德林市的实验表明，该方法能显著提升运营效率并支持可持续回收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市垃圾管理面临人口增长、垃圾种类复杂、交通拥堵和可持续基础设施需求等挑战，现有方法缺乏对设施选址与多层级路径规划的联合优化。

Method: 构建集成位置-路径模型，同时捕获设施选址决策、多产品路径结构、车辆容量限制和重复卸载操作，目标是在投资预算约束下最小化运输、路径规划和装卸等总运营成本。

Result: 基于麦德林市实际场景的计算实验表明，集成基础设施与路径决策能显著提升运营效率，支持更可持续的回收运营。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为城市规划者设计经济高效且环境负责的垃圾管理系统的实用决策支持工具。

Abstract: Urban waste management faces increasing operational and environmental challenges driven by population growth, heterogeneous waste streams, traffic congestion, and the need for sustainable collection infrastructures. We present an integrated optimization framework for the design of multi-type urban waste collection and recycling systems. In the proposed network, waste generated at distributed collection points is gathered by waste-specific small vehicles and transported to intermediate consolidation facilities, where it is aggregated before being transferred to treatment plants using larger vehicles. The problem consists of jointly determining the location of consolidation facilities and treatment plants, together with the associated two-echelon routing operations, under a limited infrastructure budget. We develop an integrated location-routing model that simultaneously captures facility-location decisions, multi-product routing structures, vehicle-capacity restrictions, and repeated unloading operations. The objective is to minimize total operational costs, including transportation, routing, and handling activities, while satisfying investment-budget constraints. The framework promotes compact collection regions, improves vehicle utilization, and reduces unnecessary transportation effort in dense urban environments. Computational experiments based on realistic urban scenarios derived from the city of Medellín (Colombia) demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. The results show that integrated infrastructure and routing decisions can substantially improve operational efficiency while supporting more sustainable recycling operations. The framework provides a practical decision-support tool for urban planners seeking to design cost-efficient and environmentally responsible waste-management systems.

</details>


### [228] [Differential Geometric Conditions for Koopman Linearizability of Control-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13577)
*Shankar A. Deka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了控制仿射非线性系统可被Koopman线性化的微分几何条件，证明了这些条件是存在Koopman线性变换的必要条件，在控制不变流形上也是充分条件，加上额外条件则成为可控线性系统Koopman线性化的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman线性化为非线性系统的控制综合与分析提供了许多可能性，但关键在于确定任意给定的非线性控制系统是否具有有限维Koopman表示，以及刻画所有可Koopman线性化的非线性控制系统的类别。

Method: 采用微分几何方法，分析控制仿射非线性系统的漂移向量场和控制向量场必须满足的条件，并讨论这些条件在控制不变流形上的充分性以及加上额外条件后的充要性。

Result: 给出了控制仿射非线性系统存在Koopman线性变换的必要微分几何条件；证明了这些条件在控制不变流形上是（稍弱意义下）Koopman线性化的充分条件；加上额外条件后成为可控制线性系统Koopman线性化的充要条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的条件易于检验，且揭示了即使自主部分可以通过Koopman提升线性化，控制仿射系统仍可能不存在Koopman线性化变换。

Abstract: Koopman linearization opens many possibilities for control synthesis and analysis of nonlinear systems. Whether or not any given nonlinear control system admits a finite-dimensional Koopman representation remains a crucial question to address. A related problem is to categorize the class of all Koopman linearizable nonlinear control systems. In this work, we present differential geometric conditions on the drift and control vector fields of a control-affine nonlinear system, that must be necessarily satisfied for Koopman linear transformation to exist. The same conditions are also shown to be sufficient for (a slightly weaker notion of) Koopman linearizability on control-invariant manifolds. Further, these conditions, together with an additional condition, become necessary and sufficient for Koopman linearizability to a controllable linear system. Our examples illustrate the ease of checking these conditions, and also shed light on how Koopman linearizing transformation may not exist for a control-affine system even though one can linearize the autonomous part of the system via Koopman lifting.

</details>


### [229] [Optimal Proximity Bound and Product Function Estimates in Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13579)
*Iskander Aliev,Gennadiy Averkov,William Jones,Timm Oertel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为整数线性规划的标准形式（max{cx: Ax=b, x≥0整数}）提供了最优邻近性界：LP松弛的最优顶点解到最近整数最优解的欧氏距离不超过√det(AAᵀ)-1，且该估计渐近紧确。同时推导了涉及乘积函数∏(xᵢ+1)的整数最优解边界，并讨论了其在背包问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为整数线性规划中LP松弛解与整数最优解之间的距离提供严格的上界估计，填补该领域在最优邻近性方面的理论空白。

Method: 通过分析A矩阵的格结构和行列式性质，利用几何数论工具推导欧氏距离上界；通过构造渐近紧确的实例证明该界的紧性；利用乘积函数∏(xᵢ+1)导出整数解的其他边界。

Result: 证明了LP松弛的最优顶点解到最近整数最优解的欧氏距离≤√det(AAᵀ)-1，且该界是渐近紧确的；得到了涉及∏(xᵢ+1)的整数解边界，并展示了在背包问题中的实际应用。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了整数线性规划最优邻近性的最优理论界，对理解LP松弛与整数解之间的差距以及设计相关算法具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We obtain an optimal proximity bound for integer linear programs in standard form max{cx: Ax=b, x nonnegative integer}, where A is an integer mxn matrix of rank m<n and b is an integer vector. Specifically, we show that the Euclidean distance from any optimal vertex solution of the LP relaxation to a nearest optimal integer solution is bounded by $\sqrt{\det(AA^t)}-1$ and that this estimate is asymptotically tight. We also derive bounds for the optimal integer solutions involving the product function $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(x_i+1)$ and discuss their applications in the knapsack setting.

</details>


### [230] [Distribution-Agnostic Robust Trajectory Optimization via Chance-Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13605)
*Yashdeep Chaudhary,Roberto Armellin,Harry Holt,Marco Sagliano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机会约束强化学习的分布无关稳健轨迹优化框架，通过离线计算标称轨迹和在线学习仿射闭环修正律（前馈控制调整+时变反馈增益）实现不确定性下的概率可行性，在三维多脉冲地火转移和随机大气定点火箭着陆两个案例中验证了其竞争力与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳健轨迹优化方法通常对不确定性分布有较强假设（如高斯），而实际任务中扰动分布未知或非高斯，亟需一种分布无关、仅需可采样的稳健优化框架，以提升方法的普适性和工程适用性。

Method: 离线计算确定性标称轨迹，再利用强化学习学习一个结构化仿射闭环修正律（前馈控制调整+时变反馈增益）。通过rollout经验上尾分位数强制执行概率可行性，通过协方差可行性惩罚调控终端散布。不确定性仅需可采样，无需分布假设。

Result: 在地火转移问题中，该方法在高斯不确定性下与最新稳健轨迹优化参考基准保持竞争力，且在均匀不确定性和未见过的过程扰动下仍有效。在随机大气定点火箭着陆问题中，该方法可迁移至短时连续推力场景（含阻力、质量消耗和下滑角约束），无需重新设计核心随机控制结构。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在保证上尾燃料成本竞争力的同时维持概率可行性，且其稳健化结构可跨异构航天器轨迹规划问题直接迁移，无需重新设计核心随机控制结构，具有良好的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a distribution-agnostic robust trajectory-optimization framework based on chance-constrained reinforcement learning. The uncertainty is represented here through initial conditions and process noise, with the only requirement being that it can be sampled. A deterministic nominal trajectory is first computed offline, and reinforcement learning is then used only to robustify that baseline through a structured affine closed-loop correction law comprising a feedforward control adjustment and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is enforced empirically through rollout-based upper-tail quantiles, while terminal dispersion is regulated through covariance-feasibility penalties. The framework is assessed on two materially different trajectory design problems. The flagship case study is a three-dimensional multi-impulse Earth-Mars transfer, where the learned policy is benchmarked against a recent robust trajectory-optimization reference under Gaussian uncertainty and then evaluated under bounded uniform uncertainty and under process disturbances not seen during training. The second case study is a stochastic atmospheric pinpoint rocket landing problem, used to assess portability to a short-horizon continuous-thrust setting with drag, mass depletion, and glide-slope constraints. The results show that the proposed framework can remain competitive in upper-tail fuel cost while preserving probabilistic feasibility, and that the same robustification scaffold can be carried across heterogeneous spacecraft trajectory planning problems without redesign of its core stochastic-control structure.

</details>
